## Summary
- **Validity verdict on file-health concerns**: mostly valid for production modules, partially overzealous for some test modules.
- **Clearly valid concerns** are concentrated in high-complexity production files:  
  `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/narrative/actions.py`,  
  `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/review/remediation.py`,  
  `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/issues.py`,  
  `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/review/import_findings.py`,  
  `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/narrative/strategy.py`.
- **Likely overzealous-but-still-useful concerns** are mostly giant test files and some cohesive “large” modules flagged mainly by LOC.
- Baseline from latest root scan: strict **95.1**, objective **96.2**, open findings **250** (`smells 141`, `structural 92`, `subjective_review 13`, `test_coverage 4`).
- Target outcome: strict **>=97.0** with measurable maintainability gains, not detector weakening.

## Public APIs / Interfaces / Types (Intentional Breaking Changes Allowed)
1. **CLI contract normalization**  
   - Canonicalize language selection to `desloppify --lang <lang> <command> ...` only.  
   - Remove ambiguous/unsupported subcommand examples such as `desloppify scan --lang python ...` from docs/help text.  
   - No compatibility shims (per hard-break policy).

2. **Internal typed interfaces (breaking for internal import consumers)**  
   - Replace broad `dict` contracts in high-churn paths with explicit dataclasses/TypedDicts for:
     - scan merge payloads,
     - narrative action inputs,
     - review import payloads,
     - work queue items.
   - Introduce typed model modules and move existing functions to consume these types.

3. **Module boundary reshaping**  
   - Split oversized orchestration files into package-local modules; keep thin facades only where necessary.
   - Break old internal import paths; update all callers and tests in the same change set.

## Phase Plan

## Phase 1: Stabilize Trust Surface (Scanner + CLI)
1. Fix scan language parsing/error path and crash path in `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/commands/_helpers.py` and `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/cli.py`.
2. Ensure root-scoped scans persist state consistently with `scan_path="."` for repo-level runs.
3. Add/adjust command tests in `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/tests/commands/` for:
   - language flag parsing,
   - error rendering for invalid runtime options,
   - root-path scan state behavior.
4. Exit criteria:
   - No CLI parsing crash paths.
   - Command tests pass.
   - Consecutive root scans produce stable state metadata and counts.

## Phase 2: Production Architecture Decomposition (Highest ROI First)
1. Decompose narrative engine:
   - Split `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/narrative/actions.py` into focused modules for action selection, impact scoring, and tool inventory.
   - Split `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/narrative/strategy.py` into lane calculation, overlap analysis, and strategy rendering.
2. Decompose review pipeline:
   - Split `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/review/import_findings.py` into parse, cache update, holistic import, and resolution modules.
   - Split `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/review/remediation.py` into plan scoring and markdown rendering modules.
3. Decompose issue/work queue/scoring core:
   - Split `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/issues.py`.
   - Split `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/work_queue.py`.
   - Split `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/scoring.py` into dimensions/policy/compute/impact components.
4. Exit criteria:
   - Production structural findings reduced by at least 40% from baseline.
   - No new regressions in command behavior.
   - Each extracted module has direct tests.

## Phase 3: Smell and Type Debt Burn-Down (Production + Script + Config)
1. Eliminate top smell classes in priority order:
   - `annotation_quality`,
   - `silent_except`,
   - `optional_param_sprawl`,
   - `swallowed_error`.
2. Standardize exception policy:
   - replace silent catches with explicit skip-reason handling and traceable context where appropriate.
3. Replace hardcoded path separators with pathlib/path-agnostic handling.
4. Convert optional-parameter-heavy functions to config objects/dataclasses.
5. Exit criteria:
   - Production `smells` findings reduced by at least 50% from baseline.
   - No silent exception handlers without explicit justification.

## Phase 4: Full Test-Suite Structural Cleanup (Because Scope = Full Cleanup)
1. Split monolithic files:
   - `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/tests/review/test_review.py`,
   - `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/tests/review/test_holistic_review.py`,
   - `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/tests/review/test_review_submodules.py`,
   - `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/tests/core/test_narrative.py`,
   into scenario-focused modules.
2. Remove test smell hotspots:
   - inline classes,
   - mutable class defaults,
   - unsafe file writes.
3. Centralize repeated fixtures/helpers into shared fixture modules under `/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/tests/`.
4. Exit criteria:
   - Structural findings in test zone reduced by at least 50%.
   - Test runtime and reliability are not degraded.

## Phase 5: Subjective Quality and Final Score Push
1. Refresh subjective coverage and elegance dimensions with current code shape.
2. Resolve all open `subjective_review` changed-file findings.
3. Run holistic re-review after major refactor batches and import findings.
4. Exit criteria:
   - strict score **>=97.0**,
   - subjective coverage debt cleared,
   - no chronic reopener findings.

## Test Cases and Scenarios
1. CLI parsing matrix:
   - valid/invalid `--lang`,
   - valid/invalid `--lang-opt`,
   - expected error codes/messages.
2. Scan-state consistency:
   - repeated root scans produce stable `scan_path`, open counts, and detector counts.
3. Functional parity tests for refactored modules:
   - narrative action ordering remains deterministic,
   - review import semantics unchanged for valid payloads,
   - score computations unchanged on fixed fixtures.
4. Regression tests for smell fixes:
   - silent-except replacements preserve skip behavior without swallowing unknown failure modes.
5. Large-suite organization tests:
   - moved/split tests still cover prior behavior paths,
   - no fixture leakage or ordering dependencies.
6. End-to-end acceptance:
   - `scan -> next -> plan -> review prepare/import -> scan` works on clean checkout without manual intervention.

## Acceptance Metrics
1. Strict score: **95.1 -> >=97.0**.
2. Open findings: **250 -> <=120**.
3. Production structural findings: **42 -> <=20**.
4. Production smell findings: **71 -> <=35**.
5. Subjective-review open changed files: **13 -> 0**.

## Assumptions and Defaults Chosen
1. Scope: **Full cleanup** (production + tests + scripts + config).
2. Refactor depth: **Aggressive redesign**.
3. Success metric: **Raise strict score to >=97**.
4. Compatibility: **Hard breaks allowed** (no CLI compatibility shims).
5. Detector policy: **Keep strict policy** (no broad threshold/exclusion tuning; fix or explicitly justify).
